I hope it will be better than the last one.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'd hoped I'd find a job, but I never did, so I was hoping you could lend me some cash.","type":"example"},{"text":"I hope {to - (that) I'll} have finished by next Sat at the latest.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To want (something) to happen, with a sense of expectation that it might [with that (+ clause); or (informal) with clause; or with so or (negative) not]."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I hope to succeed.","type":"example"},{"text":"I hope to be going back again soon.","type":"example"},{"text":"I was hoping to find a pair of jeans, but I couldn't.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To intend to do something and look forward to the prospect of having done it [with to (+ infinitive)]."],"tags":["catenative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"They're hoping for the best, but I don't think it's looking very good.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'm hoping for my boss to offer me a pay raise.","type":"example"},{"text":"He's past hoping for any improvement.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To expect optimistically that one might get something (either a change in circumstance or an object) [with for]."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To place confidence; to trust with confident expectation of good [with in]."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I hope you all the best.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To wish."],"tags":["dialectal","nonstandard","transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/həʊp/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/hoʊp/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/həʉp/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/hɐʉp/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/hop/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/hoːp/","tags":["Wales"]}],"upstream_line":210981,"upstream_offset":1018144662,"upstream_record_sha256":"67dcfc8931073e39004bd132fd90611921bc6b9e9fb4347e9d92fb50f40ecef1","word":"hope"}
{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"hopes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"All hopes for a truce are gone after the latest attack.","type":"example"},{"text":"After losing my job, there's frail hope of affording my world cruise.","type":"example"},{"text":"There is still hope that we can find our missing cat.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The feeling of trust, confidence, belief or expectation that something wished for can or will happen."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The actual thing wished for."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We still have one hope left: my roommate might see the note I left on the table.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person or thing that is a source of hope."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["The virtuous desire for future good."],"tags":["uncountable"],"topics":["Christianity"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/həʊp/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/hoʊp/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/həʉp/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/hɐʉp/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/hop/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/hoːp/","tags":["Wales"]}],"upstream_line":210982,"upstream_offset":1018195424,"upstream_record_sha256":"f060f8697b70ad12e7e98e2f27bd48d3517ca1fdb832e6de4b763ed3ab76e866","word":"hope"}
